- **code-scout**: Read-only search agent for locating code and files, tool-restricted to read/find/grep/glob. Finds files by pattern, searches for symbols or keywords, and answers "where is X defined / which files reference Y". Use for fast codebase research and broad pattern searches; hands back compressed context with path:line anchors the caller can use without re-reading everything. Not for reviews, design analysis, planning synthesis, or web research. (只读搜索代理：定位代码与文件、回答「X 在哪定义 / 哪些文件引用 Y」；快速代码库调研，回报 path:line 锚点) ([crochee/dotfiles](https://github.com/crochee/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/home/private_dot_omp/agent/agents/code-scout.md))
